## Module 08 - Method Flow, failures and recovery

The immutable evidence ledger is docs/merrin-vale/v688-v4/x2/method-flow/ledger.json. Its closeout overlay is docs/merrin-vale/v688-v4/final/method-flow-closeout-overlay.json. Together they contain 54 methods, 397 retained failed witnesses and 672 bounded passes. No failed witness is erased or retroactively promoted. Operational failures include display truncations, exact-path discovery errors, two official HTML access failures, privacy-pattern false positives, the initial ordered-speech planning defect, CRLF wheel metadata handling and the verbose evidence-commit display. Candidate output, strict-JSON, extra-field repair, skill, runner and package adverse witnesses remain independently addressable at zero original success credit.
